2026-10-20T19:01:55.8875852Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Enable hot-4 expert replication for Prefill MegaMoE

Why
The same-run control-header measurements from kernel bench 39462905249 favor four hot replicas over eight. Median MegaMoE latency is:

| Layer | k=0, 128 physical | k=4, 140 physical | k=8, 152 physical |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| 43 | 1.6252 ms | 1.4865 ms, -8.5% | 1.5866 ms, -2.4% |
| 62 | 1.5997 ms | 1.4958 ms, -6.5% | 1.5498 ms, -3.1% |
| 7 | 1.5846 ms | 1.5076 ms, -4.9% | 1.6044 ms, +1.2% |

Four replicas retain useful same-GPU dispatch while limiting physical-expert tile padding and weight traffic. Prepare serving only after the separate kb5 numerical gate has passed.

What
Restore the prepared replica-B serving changes and specialize them to k=4. Generate a 94-by-140 physical_to_logical_map from run 39051152456 logical_count using replica_plan.py. Place the four hottest logical experts once on every rank, then assign the remaining 124 experts with capacity-constrained LPT, exactly 31 cold experts per rank. Document the generation command in the planner docstring. The three representative layer maps match the kernel benchmark exactly.

Configure Prefill with twelve redundant experts and the new replica4 placement filename. Pass the redundancy option only to Prefill through the existing configuration and placement-staging path. Add megamoe-prefill-replicas.patch after the existing SGLang overlays; leave the image's original patches and DeepGEMM host untouched. Permit redundancy twelve only in Prefill with nontrivial static placement, and continue requiring zero redundancy in Decode. Allocate the symmetric buffer using the loaded physical-expert count. Derive the minimum padded rows from that count: 64 times 140 divided by EP4 times top-8 gives 280 rows. Pad both empty and tiny live Prefill ranks to that minimum while preserving the true pre-dispatch token count and slicing the returned output to live rows. Existing EPLB checkpoint loading supplies identical weights/scales to each replica; static dispatch selects the same-GPU copy.

Update the source checker, server-info checker and test expectations for 12 redundant, 140 physical and 35 local experts. Keep EPLB disabled. Decode remains unreplicated. Keep max-total-tokens 131072 and mem-fraction-static 0.90 unchanged.

Checks
Apply all configured SGLang overlays in order, without fuzz, to source extracted from the pinned Firmus image 39126563684. The base-source check and effective-source replica checks pass. Execute the actual static selector across all 94 layers and the actual FP8 checkpoint-loader fan-out for representative layers with symbolic payloads. Verify 140-slot buffer arguments, local hot-copy selection, complete unique cold ownership, all 35 weight/scale slots per rank, 280-row padding, true token counts and zero-redundancy Decode guards. Shell syntax and Ruff pass. Existing fixture data are unchanged. The complete metrics suite passes 410 tests and 22 subtests in 166.00 seconds.

Placement SHA256: 5d2ae7f6e3e262d0491a07ef71a846278fc0af995210be5dad52fb881b71f61d.
Overlay SHA256: 6f2b64cdc4f05ea31af7be60c313d5365646885afef73a3d2ce3f3a8e9846ea4.
No image rebuild or DeepGEMM host change is required. No GPU job, branch commit or push is performed. The Firmus extraction directory is made user-writable and removed.

Expected
Project approximately 6 percent lower MegaMoE time, translating to approximately 3.5 to 4 percent lower Prefill time and a 3 to 4 percent score increase. These are serving hypotheses based on three sampled layers, not measured end-to-end gains; verify TTFT at most 3 seconds and ITL at most 20 milliseconds in the serving run.

Assuming equal source-rank token counts and routing distributions, mean remote-slot fraction falls from 75 percent to 49.5636 percent across 94 layers. Aggregate rank max/mean averages 1.000003; pass-token-weighted max/mean averages 1.030144, with worst layer 1.046821. Three extra FP8 experts per GPU per layer, including FP32 block scales, add 4.958241 GiB. Against the prior minimum 62.61 GiB free after memory-pool initialization, the estimated remainder is 57.652 GiB, or 43.612 GiB beyond the ten-percent device reserve. KV capacity therefore stays unchanged.

Risks
Steady-state peak memory, allocator and weight-transformation temporaries, extra symmetric-buffer storage, GPU numerical behavior with the new gate, and serving goodput remain unmeasured. Source checks do not establish collective liveness; the mixed, idle and replay GPU gate must precede serving. Padding tiny live ranks deliberately uses the consistent Prefill geometry and scale granularity. Quantization formats and numerical tolerances remain unchanged; replicas use identical logical weights. Uneven source-rank traffic or changing routing distributions can alter locality and load balance. Decode receives no replicas, but end-to-end Decode latency still requires measurement under the changed Prefill traffic.

Gate: kernel bench 39477648172 passed the replica4 logical-128 reference /
physical-140 gate (mixed geometry, all idle, live after idle, replay), and
repeated the measured-routing pairs: layer 43 1.6008 -> 1.4849 ms, layer 62
1.6177 -> 1.5284 ms, layer 7 1.6012 -> 1.5150 ms.
